//! `check`'s advisory notes, ranked. A green check printed nine notes in
//! source order, and builders could not tell an FYI about offline coverage
//! from a page that never completes. Notes stay advisory (they never fail a
//! check); this only orders them, names each group, and shortens repeats.

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Debug)]
pub(crate) enum Tier {
    /// Can cost correctness or completeness: a page that never completes, a
    /// write no caller can pass, a disclosure, a read a device cannot answer.
    Review,
    /// Costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage.
    Performance,
    /// What a declaration implies; nothing to fix for online use.
    Design,
}

pub(crate) fn tier(code: &str) -> Tier {
    if snapback4_lang::security::note_priority(code)<=2{return Tier::Review;}
    match code {
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" | "N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL" | "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE" | "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" | "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" | "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" | "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" | "N_WRITE_DENIED" | "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" | "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" | "N_PATCH_CLEARS" | "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" | "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_VACUOUS_RULE" | "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" | "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED" | "N_UNIQUE_HIDDEN" | "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" | "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" | "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" | "N_QUERY_FILTERED" | "N_SHAPE_METER" | "N_HELD_UNKNOWN" | "N_SEQUENCE_READ"
        | "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" | "N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" | "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"
        | "N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT" | "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE" | "N_SEARCH_UNSCOPED" | "N_SEARCH_OPEN" | "N_TIMELINE_PROFILE_CAP" | "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION" | "N_SELF_CLAIMED_RELATIONSHIP" | "N_CALLER_TIME" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" | "N_ARGUMENT_GRANT" | "N_BROAD_READ" | "N_SECURITY_INTENT" => Tier::Review,
        // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.4 — a cursorless `first N` is what the declaration says: the first N rows (wave 3 #7).
        "N_SYNC_ORDER" | "N_NO_HORIZON" | "N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED" | "N_TIMELINE_COMPAT" | "N_NO_CURSOR" => Tier::Design,
        _ => Tier::Performance,
    }
}

/// Security findings that can grant authority stay ahead of repeated advice.
pub(crate) fn summary_order(code:&str)->(Tier,u8){
    let priority=snapback4_lang::security::note_priority(code);
    (if priority<=2 {Tier::Review}else{tier(code)},priority)
}

pub(crate) fn site_order(site:&str)->(String,u32,u32){
    let mut parts=site.rsplitn(3,':');let col=parts.next().and_then(|s|s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);let line=parts.next().and_then(|s|s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);let file=parts.next().unwrap_or(site);(file.to_owned(),line,col)
}

/// The first words of a note's line: what to do about it, or that nothing
/// needs doing (wave 7 #11: "put a one-line verdict on every advisory note").
// @ref LLP 3000.036#17.7 — the verdict leads; the explanation follows it.
pub(crate) fn verdict(code: &str, summary: &str, form: bool) -> &'static str {
    match code {
        "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION" => "review: restrict authority assignment",
        "N_SELF_CLAIMED_RELATIONSHIP" => "review: verify the relationship before granting access",
        "N_CALLER_TIME" => "review: use server time or bound the supplied time",
        "N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_BROAD_READ" => "review: confirm each admitted audience and row state",
        "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" => "review: answer the audience questions from the spec",
        "N_SECURITY_INTENT" => "review: write independent intent from the spec",
        "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" if summary.starts_with("persona-only:") => "no action without use identity",
        "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" => "fix: add a production bootstrap writer",
        "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" => "fix: update the fixture arguments",
        "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" => "review: complete for the pinned parent audience; other callers may be capped",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("the complete form is to add `cap ") => "fix: add the exact source cap below, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("no verified complete form for this read") => "review: no verified complete form for this read; expect `capped` and show what arrived",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form && summary.contains("the closest form is") => "fix: use the closest form below (one arm stays `complete: false`), or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form => "fix: use the complete form below, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" => "fix: pin the scope, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" => "fix: declare the reference's `on delete`, or guard the delete",
        "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" => "review: some deleters get NOT_FOUND",
        "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" => "review: this reference argument has no mandatory target lookup",
        "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" => "review: hidden and absent are different caller outcomes",
        "N_PATCH_CLEARS" => "review: an omitted argument clears this column",
        "N_WRITE_DENIED" if summary.contains("admits only 'service:") => "review: a seat require grants no table authority",
        "N_WRITE_DENIED" => "fix: this write refuses E_RULE for callers you admit",
        "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" => "review: callers you admit may still get E_RULE",
        "N_QUERY_FILTERED" => "review: this query branch may filter rows under table authority",
        "N_READ_DENIED" => "fix: this read always returns null",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" if summary.contains("get NOT_FOUND") => "review: the hidden lookup runs before this require",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" => "fix: this require cannot see the rows it tests",
        "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" => "fix: this nullable lookup is always null for the admitted callers",
        "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" => "review: a required read gate may hide rows from writers",
        "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED" => "review: confirm lookup authority and the hidden-row outcome",
        "N_UNIQUE_HIDDEN" => "review: hidden unique conflicts retain generic E_RULE",
        "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" => "review: who can read this",
        "N_VACUOUS_RULE" => "review: who can write this",
        "N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT" => "review: confirm creation authority as well as edit authority",
        "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE" => "review: confirm every result/refusal audience",
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" => "review: readable conflicts require the original immutable value",
        "N_NO_CURSOR" => "no action for a top-N list",
        _ => match tier(code) {
            Tier::Review => "review",
            Tier::Performance => "no action unless it is slow (`check --cost` measures it)",
            Tier::Design => "no action for an online app",
        },
    }
}

fn heading(tier: Tier) -> &'static str {
    match tier {
        Tier::Review => "review — can cost correctness or completeness:",
        Tier::Performance => "performance — costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage:",
        Tier::Design => "by design — what a declaration implies; no action needed for online use:",
    }
}

pub(crate) struct Note {
    pub site: String,
    pub code: String,
    pub summary: String,
    /// An expanded compiled repair: its lead line and the code. Compact
    /// completeness notes leave this empty and keep the repair in `--verbose`.
    pub form: Option<(String, String)>,
    /// Three-line default rendering for completeness verdicts. Their full
    /// planner proof and compiled repair remain in `check --verbose`.
    pub compact: Option<[String; 3]>,
}

/// The compiled form leading a note's rewrite: the text before "Otherwise:",
/// when it holds code (a lead line ending in `:` and the declaration under it).
// @ref LLP 3000.036#15.5 — the compiled owner-or-X rewrite prints by default, once per form.
pub(crate) fn compiled_form(code: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Option<(String, String)> {
    if !matches!(code, "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE") { return None; }
    let head = rewrite.split("\n\nOtherwise:").next()?;
    let (lead, body) = head.split_once('\n')?;
    (lead.ends_with(':') && body.trim_start().starts_with("query ")).then(|| (lead.to_owned(), body.trim_end().to_owned()))
}

// Audience questions and repairs differ per rule; repeated codes cannot hide them.
fn per_rule_security(code:&str)->bool {snapback4_lang::security::note_priority(code)<=2||matches!(code,"N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION"|"N_SELF_CLAIMED_RELATIONSHIP"|"N_CALLER_TIME"|"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"|"N_BROAD_READ"|"N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE")}

/// The ranked block printed after `check: ok`; empty for no notes.
pub(crate) fn render(notes: Vec<Note>) -> Vec<String> {
    if notes.is_empty() { return Vec::new(); }
    // Collapse one code's repeats at one site, keeping first-seen order.
    let mut unique: Vec<(Note, usize)> = Vec::new();
    for note in notes {
        match unique.iter_mut().find(|(seen, _)| seen.site == note.site && seen.code == note.code && seen.summary == note.summary && seen.form == note.form && seen.compact == note.compact) {
            Some((_, count)) => *count += 1,
            None => unique.push((note, 1)),
        }
    }
    let total = unique.len();
    // Rank by tier; within a tier, a code's notes stay together in the order the code first appeared.
    let first_seen = |code: &str, unique: &[(Note, usize)]| unique.iter().position(|(n, _)| n.code == code).unwrap();
    let mut order: Vec<usize> = (0..unique.len()).collect();
    order.sort_by_key(|&i| (tier(&unique[i].0.code), first_seen(&unique[i].0.code, &unique), i));
    let mut lines = vec![format!("{total} thing{} to review (advisory; check --verbose explains each, snapback4 why <CODE> one):",
        if total == 1 { "" } else { "s" })];
    let mut current = None;
    let mut shown_codes = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_forms = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_compacts = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_actions = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_context = Vec::new();
    // @ref LLP 3000.036#16.5 — wave 6 #25: a code's repeats share one line naming each subject.
    let mut repeats: Vec<(String, Vec<String>)> = Vec::new();
    let flush = |lines: &mut Vec<String>, repeats: &mut Vec<(String, Vec<String>)>| {
        for (code, subjects) in repeats.drain(..) {
            lines.push(format!("note: {code} (as above) also for {}", subjects.join("; ")));
        }
    };
    for i in order {
        let (note, count) = &unique[i];
        let group = tier(&note.code);
        if current != Some(group) { flush(&mut lines, &mut repeats); lines.push(heading(group).to_owned()); current = Some(group); }
        if note.code == "N_NO_CURSOR" {
            if !shown_codes.contains(&note.code) {
                let subjects = unique.iter().filter(|(n,_)| n.code == "N_NO_CURSOR").map(|(n,_)|
                    format!("{} ({})", subject(&n.summary), n.site)).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ");
                lines.push(format!("note: N_NO_CURSOR: no action for a top-N list. Pages without continuation: {subjects}; if the screen pages, add `c: cursor ?` and `after c`"));
                shown_codes.push(note.code.clone());
            }
            continue;
        }
        let times = if *count > 1 { format!(" (×{count} at this site)") } else { String::new() };
        let new_form = note.form.as_ref().is_some_and(|(_, code)| !shown_forms.contains(code));
        let new_compact = note.compact.as_ref().is_some_and(|parts| !shown_compacts.contains(parts));
        if shown_codes.contains(&note.code) && !new_form && !new_compact && !per_rule_security(&note.code) {
            // A repeat of a code already explained above: its subject and site, on the code's one line.
            let subject = format!("{} ({}){times}", subject(&note.summary), note.site);
            match repeats.iter_mut().find(|(code, _)| *code == note.code) {
                Some((_, subjects)) => subjects.push(subject),
                None => repeats.push((note.code.clone(), vec![subject])),
            }
            continue;
        }
        flush(&mut lines, &mut repeats);
        if let Some(parts @ [action, impact, audience]) = &note.compact {
            if !shown_actions.contains(action) {
                let mut body = action.lines();
                lines.push(format!("note: {} — {} — {}{times}", note.site, note.code, body.next().unwrap_or("review")));
                lines.extend(body.map(str::to_owned));
                shown_actions.push(action.clone());
            } else {
                lines.push(format!("note: {} — {} — {}: checked rewrite or limitation as above{times}", note.site, note.code, subject(&note.summary)));
            }
            for context in [impact, audience] {
                if !shown_context.contains(&(note.code.clone(), context.clone())) {
                    lines.push(format!("  {context}"));
                    shown_context.push((note.code.clone(), context.clone()));
                }
            }
            shown_compacts.push(parts.clone());
            shown_codes.push(note.code.clone());
            continue;
        }
        if shown_codes.contains(&note.code) && !per_rule_security(&note.code) {
            // A repeat that carries a form not printed yet keeps its own line, then the form.
            lines.push(format!("note: {} — {}: (as above) {}{times}", note.site, note.code, short(&note.summary)));
        } else {
            let below = note.form.as_ref().is_some_and(|(_, code)| !shown_forms.contains(code));
            let summary = match note.summary.strip_suffix(" (check --verbose)") { Some(text) if below => format!("{text} (below)"), _ => note.summary.clone() };
            lines.push(format!("note: {} — {}: {}. {summary}{times}", note.site, note.code, verdict(&note.code, &note.summary, below)));
            shown_codes.push(note.code.clone());
        }
        if let Some((lead, code)) = &note.form && !shown_forms.contains(code) {
            lines.push(format!("  rewrite: {lead}"));
            lines.extend(code.lines().map(|line| format!("    {line}")));
            shown_forms.push(code.clone());
        format!("review {subject}: analyzer limitation; no verified complete rewrite; keep the query and handle partial data (check --verbose explains why)")
    } else if note.rewrite.starts_with("add `cap ") {
        format!("rewrite for {subject}: {}", note.rewrite)
    } else if let Some(columns) = note.message.split("` reads ").nth(1).and_then(|tail| tail.split_once(", which the scan does not pin").map(|(columns,_)| columns)) {
        format!("review {subject}: missing pin {columns}; choose the intended scope without dropping other readers (check --verbose)")
    } else {
        format!("review {subject}: no checked rewrite; keep the query and handle partial data (check --verbose)")
    };
    let admitted = ["for callers `", "except for callers `"].into_iter().find_map(|needle| {
        note.message.split_once(needle)?.1.split_once("` admits").map(|(rule, _)| rule)
    });
    let mut who = match admitted {
        Some(rule) => format!("audience: `{rule}` is proved complete; other callers may be capped"),
        None if note.code == "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" => "audience: pinned parent readers are proved complete; other callers may be capped".into(),
        None => "audience: completeness is not proved for every caller; runtime complete may still be true".into(),
    };
    if let Some(scan) = note.message.split_once(" over ").and_then(|(_, tail)| tail.split_once(" is ").map(|(scan, _)| scan)) {
        who.push_str(&format!(" (scan {scan})"));
    }
    Some([action, "impact: fine for a screen showing returned rows; proving absence or all matches needs complete: true; keep partial data visible".into(), who])
}

/// `check --emit-lean <file.q>`: one file, checked, as the Lean model's
/// schema, helpers and programs (`snapback4_lang::emit::lean`).
// @ref LLP 3000.035#52-stage-2-a-small-lean-core — the emitter's command-line face; the harness calls the library directly
fn emit_lean(file: &str) -> i32 {
    let source = match std::fs::read_to_string(file) {
        Ok(source) => source,
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("cannot read {file}: {error}"); return 2; }
    };
    let name = Path::new(file).file_name().and_then(|n| n.to_str()).unwrap_or(file).to_owned();
    match snapback4_lang::emit::lean::lean(&[(name, source)]) {
        Ok(module) => { println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&module.to_json()).unwrap()); 0 }
        Err(snapback4_lang::emit::lean::LeanError::Diagnostics(problems)) => {
            for problem in problems { eprintln!("{problem}"); }
            1
        }
        Err(snapback4_lang::emit::lean::LeanError::Unsupported(u)) => {
            eprintln!("outside the Lean model's fragment: {u}");
            1
        }
    }
}

pub fn check(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    if options.flags.contains_key("audit") { return security::audit(options); }
    if options.flags.contains_key("store") && options.flags.contains_key("release") {
        return releases::check_release(options);
    }

    if let Some(file) = options.flags.get("emit-lean") {
        return emit_lean(file);
    }
    let project = project_or_exit();
    let started = Instant::now();
    package_versions::warn(&project);
    upgrade_history::report(&project, options.json);
    if options.flags.contains_key("fix") {
        if let Err(code) = fix::checked(fix::compile(&project)) { return code; }
    }
    let compiled_sources = match project.sources() {
        Ok(sources) => sources,
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("cannot read snapback/: {error}"); return 2; }
    };
    let mut bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&compiled_sources);
    let reviewable_notes = bound.notes.iter().map(note_reviews::fingerprint).collect();
    for warning in saved_fixture_drift::warnings(&project) {
        if warning.drift { eprintln!("check: WARNING: saved fixture drift: {}", warning.message); }
        else { eprintln!("check: info: {}", warning.message); }
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("fix") && bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        if let Err(error) = fix_fixtures::run(&project, &bound, options) { failed_check(&bound, options.json, "E_INPUT", format!("check --fix: {error}")); return 2; }
    }

    if let Some(hint) = project.saved_copy_hint() { if options.json { eprintln!("check: {hint}"); } else { println!("check: {hint}"); } }
    if let Some(error) = crate::exact_device::inspect(&project.root, &bound.schema).into_iter().find_map(Result::err) {
        if options.json { println!("{}", json!({"ok": false, "denied": {"code": "E_DEVICE_VERSION", "message": error}, "notes": bound.notes, "newNotes": bound.notes, "acknowledgedNotes": []})); }
        else { eprintln!("{error}"); }
        return 1;
    }
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        check_surface::report(&project, &bound, options.json);
        report_diagnostics(&bound, options.json, true, options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
        if !options.json { print_prediction(&bound, options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
        return 1;
    }
    let security = security::report(&project, &bound, &compiled_sources, options);
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.3 — check judges adoption against the dev store's active generation and rows, read-only
    match adoption::diagnostics(&project, &bound.schema, options.flags.get("store"), options.json || options.flags.contains_key("verbose")) {
        Ok((problems, notes)) => { bound.diagnostics.extend(problems); bound.notes.extend(notes); },
        Err(error) => { failed_check(&bound, options.json, "E_STORE", format!("cannot check store adoption: {error}")); return 2; }
    }
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        check_surface::report(&project, &bound, options.json);
        report_diagnostics(&bound, options.json, true, options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
        if !options.json { print_prediction(&bound, options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
        return 1;
    }
    for diagnostic in readiness::notes(&project, &bound, options.flags.get("fixture")) {
        if diagnostic.code == "E_FIXTURE_TIME" { bound.diagnostics.push(diagnostic); }
        else { bound.notes.push(diagnostic); }
    }
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        report_diagnostics(&bound, options.json, true, options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
        return 1;
    }
    bound.notes.extend(snapback4_lang::bind::residency::change_notes(&bound, &residency::previous(&project)));
    check_surface::report(&project, &bound, options.json);
    if let Err(error) = project.write_generated(&bound) {
        failed_check(&bound, options.json, "E_STORE", format!("cannot write generated types: {error}"));
        return 2;
    }
    if let Err(error) = project.check_server() {
        if options.json { println!("{}", json!({ "ok": false, "problems": [{ "code": if error.starts_with("E_SERVER_TOOLCHAIN") { "E_SERVER_TOOLCHAIN" } else { "E_SERVER_TYPECHECK" }, "message": error, "guide": "actions" }], "notes": bound.notes, "newNotes": bound.notes, "acknowledgedNotes": [] })); }
        else { eprintln!("{error}"); }
        return 1;
    }
    // A native job's module is built here too (LLP 3000.005): the schema's
    // Rust types written, cargo run, the module checked against the jobs.
    let native = match crate::native::build(&project, &bound) {
        Ok(built) => built,
        Err(problem) => {
            if options.json {
                println!("{}", json!({ "ok": false, "problems": [{ "code": problem.code, "message": problem.message, "rewrite": problem.rewrite, "guide": "native" }], "notes": bound.notes, "newNotes": bound.notes, "acknowledgedNotes": [] }));
            } else {
                eprintln!("{problem}\n");
                eprintln!("check: 1 problem");
            }
            return 1;
        }
    };
    check_surface::remember(&project, &bound);
    if let Err(error) = residency::remember(&project, &bound) { failed_check(&bound, options.json, "E_STORE", format!("cannot write residency facts: {error}")); return 2; }
    let acquisition = if options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
        match residency::at_check(&project, &bound, options) {
            Ok(report) => report,
            Err(error) => { failed_check(&bound, options.json, "E_INPUT", format!("residency measurement failed: {error}")); return 1; }
        }
    } else { None };
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#132-reviewed-notes — every failed check preserves review evidence, including independent policy refutations.
    let reviewed = if security.refuted() {
        note_reviews::Review { new: bound.notes.clone(), acknowledged: Vec::new() }
    } else { match note_reviews::review(&project, &compiled_sources, &bound.notes, &reviewable_notes, options.flags.contains_key("ack-notes")) {
        Ok(review) => review,
        Err(error) => {
            if options.json { println!("{}", json!({"ok": false, "problems": [{"code": "E_CHECK_NOTES", "message": error}], "notes": bound.notes, "newNotes": bound.notes, "acknowledgedNotes": []})); }
            else { eprintln!("check: E_CHECK_NOTES: {error}"); }
            return 2;
        }
    } };
    let dispositions = match security_reviews::review(&project,&compiled_sources,&security.notes,
        if security.refuted(){None}else{options.flags.get("ack-security").map(|id|(id.as_str(),options.flags.get("reason").map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("")))}) {
        Ok(d)=>d,Err(error)=>{if options.json {println!("{}",json!({"ok":false,"security":&security,"problems":[{"code":"E_SECURITY_NOTES","message":error}]}));}else{eprintln!("check: E_SECURITY_NOTES: {error}");}return 2;}
    };
    let security_failed=security.refuted()||security_reviews::pending(&dispositions);
    let visible_security=security.notes.iter().zip(&dispositions).filter(|(_,d)|!d.acknowledged).map(|(n,_)|n).collect::<Vec<_>>();
//! Expand advisory write sites with lexical arguments and dominating guards.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#128-runtime-intent-and-independent-worlds — helper placement cannot hide a grant writer.
use super::*;
use ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramDecl,FunctionBody};
type Env=BTreeMap<String,Expr>;
fn resolve(expr:&Expr,env:&Env)->Expr{
    let mut expr=expr.clone();
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|{
        if let Expr::Object{fields,..}|Expr::Write{fields,..}=e{for f in fields{if f.value.is_none(){f.value=Some(env.get(&f.name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Expr::Name{name:f.name.clone(),site:f.site.clone()}));}}}
        if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e&&let Some(value)=env.get(name){*e=value.clone();}
    });expr
}
fn expression(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,guards:&[Stmt],depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    let mut expr=resolve(expr,env);
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|match e{
        Expr::Write{..}=>{let mut context=p.clone();context.prelude=Vec::new();context.body=guards.to_vec();out.push((e.clone(),context));},
        Expr::Call{name,args,..} if depth<32=>if let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(name){
            let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(arg,value)|(arg.name.clone(),value.clone())).collect();
            let mut inherited=guards.to_vec();
            match &f.body{
                FunctionBody::Expression(value)=>expression(catalog,p,value,&locals,&inherited,depth+1,out),
                FunctionBody::Block(body)=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut locals,&mut inherited,depth+1,out),
            }
        },_=>{}
    });
}
// Only an unconditional direct call exports a helper's guaranteed prefix.
// Conditional/loop-local requirements never become caller assumptions.
fn helper_guards(catalog:&Catalog,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,depth:usize)->Vec<Stmt>{
    if depth>=32{return Vec::new();}
    let Expr::Call{name,args,..}=resolve(expr,env)else{return Vec::new();};
    let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(&name)else{return Vec::new();};
    let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(a,v)|(a.name.clone(),v)).collect::<Env>();
    let FunctionBody::Block(body)=&f.body else{return if let FunctionBody::Expression(value)=&f.body{helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)}else{Vec::new()};};
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in body{match s{
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>out.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,&locals),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()}),
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));if names.len()==1{locals.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,&locals));}},
        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)),
        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));break;},_=>break,
    }}out
}
fn statements(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,body:&[Stmt],env:&mut Env,guards:&mut Vec<Stmt>,depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    for s in body{match s{
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));if names.len()==1{env.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,env));}},
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>{
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
            guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,env),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()});
        },
        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);break;},
        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));},
        Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,site}=>{
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
            let condition=resolve(condition,env);
            let mut then_guards=guards.clone();
            then_guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:condition.clone(),code:"$advice".into(),message:None,site:site.clone()});
            statements(catalog,p,then_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut then_guards,depth,out);
            let mut else_guards=guards.clone();
            else_guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:Expr::Unary{op:"not".into(),value:Box::new(condition),site:site.clone()},code:"$advice".into(),message:None,site:site.clone()});
            statements(catalog,p,else_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut else_guards,depth,out);
        },
        Stmt::For{domain,name,body,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,domain,env,guards,depth,out);let mut local=env.clone();local.remove(name);statements(catalog,p,body,&mut local,&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);},
        Stmt::As{body,..}=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out),_=>{}
    }}
}
pub(super) fn writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut env=Env::new();let mut guards=Vec::new();
    statements(catalog,p,&p.prelude,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);
    statements(catalog,p,&p.body,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);out
}

//! Deterministic role populations validated by the ordinary seed boundary.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#132-generated-initial-worlds-and-password-only-test — generated initialization is explicit.
use super::*;
/// No network, credentials, development doors, durable store or scratch files.
fn proposals(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign)->Result<Engine,String>{
    if !out.reserve(1){return Err("generated fixture world not reserved; remaining initialization uncovered".into());}
    let mut people=Vec::new();
    for (table,_) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        let role_columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
        for column in role_columns{
            {
                for role in super::super::privilege::role_values(bound,table,&column) {for n in 0..2 {people.push((table.clone(),column.clone(),role.clone(),format!("account:security-{table}-{role}-{n}")));}}
            }
        }
    }
    for seat in histories::viewers(bound).into_iter().filter(|p|p.starts_with("service:")&&p!="service:security") {for n in 0..2{people.push((String::new(),String::new(),String::new(),format!("account:security-{}-{n}",seat.replace(':',"-"))));}}
    if people.is_empty(){for n in 0..2{people.push((String::new(),String::new(),String::new(),format!("account:security-owner-{n}")));}}
    if people.len()>64{people.truncate(64);out.unsupported.insert("generated roster capped at 64 principals; additional roles remain uncovered".into());}
    let mut grant_tables=BTreeSet::new();
    fn grants(rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|v|matches!(v,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{out.insert(table.clone());},
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{grants(r,out)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>grants(r,out),_=>{}
    }}
    for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for rule in std::iter::once(&t.rules.read).chain(t.column_reads.values()){grants(rule,&mut grant_tables);}}
    let counts=bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(table,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()).map(|(table,t)|{
        let own=people.iter().filter(|(roster,_,_,_)|roster==table).count();(table.clone(),if own>0{own}else if grant_tables.contains(table)||std::iter::once(&t.rules.read).chain(t.column_reads.values()).any(|rule|rule.any_term(&|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if t.columns.get(c).is_some_and(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal))))){people.len().max(1usize<<t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count().min(3))}else{t.columns.values().filter_map(|k|if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){Some(variants.len())}else{None}).max().unwrap_or(2).max(1usize<<t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count().min(3)).clamp(2,8)})
    }).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    let mut rows=BTreeMap::new();
    for (table,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        if !counts.contains_key(table){continue;}
        let own=people.iter().filter(|(roster,_,_,_)|roster==table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count()>3 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: full Cartesian combinations above three principal columns remain uncovered"));}
        let mut population=Vec::new();
        for n in 0..counts[table]{
            let person=if own.is_empty(){&people[n%people.len()]}else{own[n]};
            let mut fields=BTreeMap::from([("id".into(),Value::Id(format!("security-{table}-{n}")))]);
            let mut principal=0;
            for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
                if column=="id"||t.engine_field(column)||t.recorded.contains_key(column)||t.maintained.contains_key(column){continue;}
                let mut value=if kind.is_optional()&&n%2==0&&!(person.0==*table&&person.1==*column){Value::Null}else{match sample(kind.required(),4+n){Some(value)=>value,None=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}.{column}: unsupported typed sample"));continue;}}};
                if value==Value::Null&&kind.is_optional(){fields.insert(column.clone(),value);continue;}
                match kind.required(){
                    C::Principal=>{
                        let linked=!own.is_empty()||grant_tables.contains(table)||t.constraints.iter().any(|constraint|matches!(constraint,Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{key,..} if key.iter().any(|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c==column))));
                        value=Value::Id(if linked{if principal==0{person.3.clone()}else{people[(n+principal)%people.len()].3.clone()}}else{if (n>>principal.min(5))&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()});principal+=1;
                    },
                    C::Ref{table:target}=>{value=Value::Id(format!("security-{target}-{}",n%counts.get(target).copied().unwrap_or(1)));},
                    C::Enum{..}|C::Text{..} if person.0==*table&&person.1==*column=>value=Value::String(person.2.clone()),
                    C::Text{max}=>{let minimum=t.constraints.iter().filter_map(|c|if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=c{(field==column).then_some(*min as usize)}else{None}).max().unwrap_or(1);let suffix=format!("sample{n}");value=Value::String(format!("{suffix}{}","x".repeat(minimum.saturating_sub(suffix.len()))).chars().take(*max as usize).collect());},
                    C::Time=>value=Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_deref()==Some(column){86_400_000}else{-1000}),
                    _=>{},
                }
                fields.insert(column.clone(),value);
            }
            population.push(Value::Row(fields.into()));
        }
        rows.insert(table.clone(),population);
    }
    // Batch validation preserves final-exists, parent ordering and maintains.
    if let Ok(engine)=engine_with(bound,&rows,Vec::new()){return Ok(engine);}
    if !out.reserve(1){return Err("generated fallback world not reserved; initialization uncovered".into());}
    let mut engine=engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new())?;
    let mut pending=rows.into_iter().flat_map(|(table,rows)|rows.into_iter().map(move|row|(table.clone(),row))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut reasons=BTreeMap::new();
    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len(){
        let before=pending.len();let mut retry=Vec::new();
        for (table,mut row) in pending {
            if !out.reserve(1){validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
            let attempt=(||{
                for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[&table].columns {if let Ok(value)=row.field(column){let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,value)?;if let Value::Row(fields)=&mut row{fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}}}
                engine.seed_at([(table.clone(),row.clone())],NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;Ok::<_,String>(())
            })();
            if let Err(why)=attempt{reasons.insert(table.clone(),why);retry.push((table,row));}
        }
        pending=retry;if pending.len()==before{break;}
    }
    for (table,why) in reasons{if pending.iter().any(|(name,_)|name==&table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: some related rows declined by ordinary validation: {why}"));}}
    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;
    Ok(engine)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — creator guards/copies are part of generated initialization.
pub fn fixture(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign)->Result<Engine,String>{
    let proposed=proposals(bound,out)?;
    let rows=readback(&proposed)?;
    let actors=histories::actors(bound,&rows);
    // A principal key and a field named role are insufficient: payload tables
    // must execute their creators even when they happen to have that shape.
    fn authority_roster(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->bool{
        let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name);
        fn used(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,name:&str,columns:&BTreeSet<String>)->bool{match rule{
            RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if table==name&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|v|matches!(v,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>super::super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,v)|columns.contains(c)&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(_)))),
            RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|r|used(bound,r,name,columns)),RuleExpr::Not(r)=>used(bound,r,name,columns),_=>false,
        }}
        !columns.is_empty()&&bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].into_iter().chain(t.column_reads.values()).any(|r|used(bound,r,name,&columns)))
    }
    let rosters=bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(name,_)|authority_roster(bound,name)).map(|(name,_)|name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut creators=bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native&&!bound.schema.operations.get(&p.program.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral)&&ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert"))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    creators.sort_by_key(|p|ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"update"|"upsert"|"delete")));
    let created=creators.iter().flat_map(|p|ir::write_tables(&p.program)).filter(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert")).map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut engine=engine_on_store(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new(),proposed.store().empty_with_asset_ledger().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)?;
    let mut pending=rows.iter().filter(|(t,_)|rosters.contains(*t)||!created.contains(*t)).flat_map(|(t,rs)|rs.iter().map(move|r|(t.clone(),r.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    // Bootstrap proposals require an actor admitted by the insert rule in the
    // previous world. Never let a proposed role authorize its own insertion.
    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len(){
        let before=pending.len();let mut retry=Vec::new();
        for (table,row) in pending{
            if !out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("generated initialization work exhausted; remaining populations uncovered".into());validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
            let seeded=clean_row(bound,&table,&row);
            let mut accepted=None;
            for actor in &actors{
                if !out.reserve(1){break;}
                // This only orders ordinary replay work: a definitely denied
                // pre-insert gate cannot be rescued by copying the entire
                // ledger. Unknown and defaults still go through the engine.
                if matches!(engine.fixture_policy(&bound.schema.tables[&table].rules.insert,actor,None,&seeded,NOW),Ok(rules::Verdict::Denied)){continue;}
                let mut candidate=engine.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
                if matches!(candidate.insert_fixture(&table,seeded.clone(),actor,NOW),Ok(r) if matches!(r.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})){accepted=Some(candidate);break;}
            }
            if let Some(candidate)=accepted{engine=candidate;}else{retry.push((table,row));}
        }
        pending=retry;if pending.len()==before{break;}
    }
    for (table,_) in pending{out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: no admitted insert/bootstrap; population uncovered"));}
    // Try principals in the admitted authority rosters before outsider probes.
    // This only orders candidates: every creator and recipient guard still
    // executes normally, and outsider actors remain in the finite campaign.
    // Avoid spending the short check budget hydrating media for absent roles.
    let mut creator_actors=actors.clone();
    let admitted=readback(&engine)?;
    let roster_principals=rosters.iter().flat_map(|name|admitted.get(name).into_iter().flatten().flat_map(|row|{
        bound.schema.tables[name].columns.iter().filter_map(move |(column,kind)|{
            if matches!(kind.required(),C::Principal){match row.field(column){Ok(Value::Id(p))=>Some(p.clone()),_=>None}}else{None}
        })
    })).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    creator_actors.sort_by_key(|actor|!roster_principals.contains(actor));
    // Authored insertion bodies preserve requires, immutable copies, defaults,
    // role checks on principal arguments and multi-table creation invariants.
    let mut attempts=0usize;let mut root_observed=false;
    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len().min(12){
        let before=readback(&engine)?;
        for bp in &creators{
            let p=&bp.program;let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
            // Preserve a pre-transition root population. Later derived creators
            // can otherwise consume every pending lifecycle row during setup.
            if !root_observed&&writes.iter().any(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"update"|"upsert"|"delete")){
                root_observed=true;
                if out.generated_fixture&&out.work_remaining.is_none()&&validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine).is_ok(){
                    let first=out.violations.len();histories::lifecycle_observers(bound,&engine,out);
                    for witness in &mut out.violations[first..]{if witness.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace{witness.reachability=Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace;witness.scope="actual authored observation from a validated generated root fixture before derived creator transitions; initialization is not claimed application-reachable".into();}}
                }
            }
            let implicit=stability::implicit(bound,p);
            if writes.iter().all(|(t,_)|rosters.contains(t)){continue;}
            let current=readback(&engine)?;
            let targets=writes.iter().filter(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert")).map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            if targets.iter().all(|t|current.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>=2){continue;}
            let cases=histories::fixture_cases(bound,p,&current,&creator_actors,out);
            'cases:for args in cases.into_iter().take(16){for actor in &creator_actors{
                if attempts>=2048||!out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("generated authored initialization capped at 2048 calls; remaining creators uncovered".into());validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
                attempts+=1;
                if targets.len()==1{
                    let gate=&bound.schema.tables[targets[0]].rules.insert;
                    let stable=!gate.any_term(&|term|!matches!(term,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_)))
                        &&kernel::dependencies(gate).iter().all(|dep|!implicit.contains(dep)&&!writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==dep));
                    if stable&&matches!(engine.fixture_policy(gate,actor,None,&Value::Null,NOW),Ok(rules::Verdict::Denied)){continue;}
                }
                let mut candidate=engine.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
                let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut candidate,&p.args,&args,actor){Ok(a)=>a,Err(_)=>continue};
                let result=candidate.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(actor,args).at(NOW));
                if matches!(result,Ok(r) if matches!(r.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})) {
                    let after=readback(&candidate)?;
                    if targets.iter().any(|t|after.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>current.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)) {engine=candidate;}
                    let after=readback(&engine)?;
                    if targets.iter().all(|t|after.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>=2){break 'cases;}
                }
            }}
        }
        if readback(&engine)?==before{break;}
    }
    for table in created.difference(&rosters){if engine.store().rows(table).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.is_empty(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: authored creator did not populate this table"));}}
    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;Ok(engine)
}
fn clean_row(bound:&Bound,table:&str,row:&Value)->Value{
    let t=&bound.schema.tables[table];
//! Concrete, isolated challenges; finite campaigns never establish proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#4-counterexamples-and-generated-tests — only replayed admission is a refutation.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::{Engine,Store,Context,Value,RuntimeError};
use snapback4_core::schema::{ColumnType as C,Constraint};
use snapback4_core::engine::rules::{self,Probe};
use snapback4_core::ir::{self,Expr,Stmt,Program,ProgramKind,Read,KeyProvenance};
pub const NOW:i64=1_800_000_000_000;
const VIEWER:&str="dev:security-a";
const OTHER:&str="dev:security-b";
// Same verified 1x1 PNG header used by the existing packaged asset witnesses.
const PNG:[u8;24]=[137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10,0,0,0,13,73,72,68,82,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1];

#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize,PartialEq,Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all="kebab-case")]
pub enum Reachability { AuthoredFixtureTrace, GeneratedFixtureTrace, TrustedSeedOnly, SyntheticOperation }
impl Reachability {
    pub fn is_reachable(&self)->bool {matches!(self,Self::AuthoredFixtureTrace|Self::GeneratedFixtureTrace)}
}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Counterexample {pub reachability:Reachability,pub invariant_site:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub viewer:String,pub operation:String,pub effect:String,pub row:Value,pub old:Option<Value>,pub supporting_rows:BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,pub rule_path:String,pub scope:String, pub observations:Option<paired::Observations>,pub history:Vec<histories::Step>}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Default,Serialize)]
pub struct Campaign {pub directed_attempts:usize,pub directed_peak_worlds:usize,#[serde(skip)] pub history_limit:usize,#[serde(skip)] pub generated_fixture:bool,#[serde(skip)] pub work_remaining:Option<usize>,#[serde(skip)] pub deadline:Option<std::time::Instant>,pub paired_observer_calls:usize,pub history_observer_calls:usize,pub column_observer_calls:usize,pub operations:BTreeMap<String,usize>,pub sequences:usize,pub attempted:usize,pub admitted:usize,pub refused:usize,pub violations:Vec<Counterexample>,pub boundary_failures:Vec<String>,pub unsupported:BTreeSet<String>}
impl Campaign {
    pub fn bounded(milliseconds:u64)->Self {Self{work_remaining:Some(512),deadline:Some(std::time::Instant::now()+std::time::Duration::from_millis(milliseconds)),..Self::default()}}
    pub(super) fn reserve(&mut self,cost:usize)->bool {
        if self.exhausted(){return false;}
        if let Some(remaining)=&mut self.work_remaining {
            if *remaining<cost{*remaining=0;self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration work budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());return false;}
            *remaining-=cost;
        }true
    }
    pub(super) fn exhausted(&mut self)->bool {
        if self.work_remaining==Some(0){self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration work budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());return true;}
        if self.deadline.is_some_and(|deadline|std::time::Instant::now()>=deadline){self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration time budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());true}else{false}
    }
}
fn id(table:&str)->Value {Value::Id(format!("security-{table}"))}
fn sample(kind:&C,variant:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match kind{
    C::Optional(inner)=>if variant&4!=0{sample(inner,variant).unwrap_or(Value::Null)}else{Value::Null},C::Id|C::Principal=>Value::Id(if variant&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()),
    C::Ref{table}=>id(table),C::Text{max}=>Value::String("x".repeat((*max).min(20) as usize)),
    C::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(variant%variants.len())?.clone()),
    C::Int|C::Decimal{..}|C::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),C::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),
    C::Bool=>Value::Bool((variant>>2)&1==0),C::Time=>Value::Int(NOW-1000),C::Json{..}=>Value::Null,C::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),
    C::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),C::ListRange{of,min,..}=>Value::Array((0..*min).map(|_|sample(of,variant)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
    C::Image{..}=>Value::String("$security:image".into()),C::File{..}=>Value::String("$security:file".into()),C::Video{..}=>return None,
})}
fn needed(schema:&Schema,name:&str,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){
    if !out.insert(name.into()){return;}let Some(t)=schema.table(name)else{return};
    for m in schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.target==name){needed(schema,&m.source,out);}
    for k in t.columns.values(){if let C::Ref{table}=k.required(){needed(schema,table,out);}}
    for r in super::columns::rules(t){for dep in kernel::dependencies(r){needed(schema,&dep,out);}}
}
fn snapshot(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    snapshot_mode(bound,inv,variant,false)
}
fn snapshot_mode(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize,timed:bool)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    let mut names=BTreeSet::new();needed(&bound.schema,&inv.table,&mut names);
    if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy{for dep in kernel::dependencies(policy){needed(&bound.schema,&dep,&mut names);}}
    if let Some(mut syntax)=inv.syntax.clone(){super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut syntax,&mut |e| {
        if let ast::Expr::Read(r)|ast::Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=e {needed(&bound.schema,&r.table,&mut names);}
    });}
    if let Some(op)=&inv.operation && let Some(p)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==op){for kind in p.program.args.values(){if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind{needed(&bound.schema,table,&mut names);}}}
    let mut out=BTreeMap::new();
    let mut enum_case=variant;
    for name in names {
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&name];
        if bound.schema.maintain_for(&name).is_some(){continue;}
        let mut fields=BTreeMap::new();fields.insert("id".into(),id(&name));
        let mut principal_index=0usize;
        for (n,k) in &t.columns{
            if n=="id"||t.engine_field(n)||t.maintained.contains_key(n){continue;}
            let sample_variant=if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){let n=enum_case % variants.len();enum_case/=variants.len();n}else if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal){let n=((variant>>((if name==inv.table{0}else{3})+principal_index.min(5)))&1)|(variant&4);principal_index+=1;n}else{variant};
            let value=sample(k,sample_variant)
                .ok_or_else(||format!("{name}.{n}: asset ledger/required sample is not available"))?;
            fields.insert(n.clone(),value);
        }
        if timed || t.read_lifetime_sensitive() {for (n,k) in &t.columns {if matches!(k.required(),C::Time)&&(!k.is_optional()||variant&4!=0) {
            fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_ref()==Some(n){2000}else if timed{1000}else{-1000}));
        }}}
        for constraint in &t.constraints{if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=constraint
            && matches!(fields.get(field),Some(Value::String(_))) {fields.insert(field.clone(),Value::String("x".repeat((*min).max(1) as usize)));}}
        // Named primary keys used by role probes must select the challenged
        // viewer; changing the enum represents a current-role change while
        // derived assignment/membership rows remain present.
        if name!=inv.table {for (i,(n,k)) in t.columns.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k,C::Principal)).enumerate(){fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Id(if variant & (1 << (i+3)) == 0 {VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into())); let _=k;}}
        out.insert(name,vec![Value::Row(fields.into())]);
    }
    Ok(out)
}
struct Snapshot<'a>{schema:&'a Schema,rows:&'a BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>}
impl Probe for Snapshot<'_>{
    fn exists(&mut self,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[Value],equalities:&[(String,Value)])->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{
        let t=&self.schema.tables[table];let Some(idx)=t.index(index)else{return Ok(false)};
        if idx.components.len()!=key.len(){return Ok(false);}
        if idx.unique&&idx.components.iter().any(|c|c.column().is_some_and(|n|t.columns.get(n).is_some_and(C::is_optional)))&&key.contains(&Value::Null){return Ok(false);}
        Ok(self.rows.get(table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|live(t,r)).any(|r|idx.components.iter().zip(key).all(|(c,v)|c.column().is_some_and(|n|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v)))&&equalities.iter().all(|(n,v)|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v))))
    }
    fn created(&mut self,_:&str,_:&str,_:&[Value])->bool{false}

}
fn live(t:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,row:&Value)->bool{
    !t.expired(row,NOW)&&t.not_before.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|match row.field(c){Ok(Value::Int(n))=>*n<=NOW,Ok(Value::Null)=>true,_=>false})
}
fn policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value)->Result<bool,String>{

    let Some(p)=inv.policy.as_ref() else{return inv.syntax.as_ref().and_then(|e|super::concrete::evaluate(e,row,rows,&bound.schema,viewer)).ok_or_else(||"concrete policy expression is unknown/unsupported (including clock and typed literal coercions)".into());};

    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
    if p.has_reference(){return Err("reference intent requires authorized engine policy replay".into());}
    rules::evaluate(p,viewer,old,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows}).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
fn policy_in(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,engine:&mut Engine)->Result<bool,String>{
    let Some(p)=&inv.policy else{return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);};
    if !p.has_reference(){return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);}
    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
    match engine.fixture_policy(p,viewer,old,&image,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?{
        rules::Verdict::Readable=>Ok(true),rules::Verdict::Denied=>Ok(false),rules::Verdict::Unknown=>Err("policy reference/coverage judgment is unknown; never a refutation".into()),
    }
}
