//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
use super::*;
const LIMIT:usize=512;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}

pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    out.into_iter().collect()
}
pub(super) fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    }}}
    out
}
pub(super) fn fixture_cases(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    let args=&p.args;
    let mut literals=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<Value>>::new();
    fn collect(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>){
        if let Some(binary)=value.get("Binary") {for (a,b) in [("left","right"),("right","left")] {
            if let (Some(name),Some(literal))=(binary.get(a).and_then(|v|v.get("Variable")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binary.get(b).and_then(|v|v.get("Literal"))) {
                if let Ok(value)=serde_json::from_value::<Value>(literal.clone()){out.entry(name.into()).or_default().push(value);}
            }
        }}
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for v in xs.values(){collect(v,out)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for v in xs{collect(v,out)},_=>{}}
    }
    collect(&serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap(),&mut literals);
    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
        Some(match k{
            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..}=>['z','y'].into_iter().map(|c|Value::String(c.to_string().repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize))).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Int=>vec![Value::Int(0),Value::Int(1),Value::Int(2)],
            ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max}=>{let mut xs=vec![*min,1i64.clamp(*min,*max),*max];xs.sort();xs.dedup();xs.into_iter().map(Value::Int).collect()},
            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
            ir::ArgType::Time=>vec![Value::Int(NOW-86_400_000),Value::Int(NOW),Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000)],
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
        })
    }
    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    for (name,k) in args{
        let Some(mut vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
        for literal in literals.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
            let valid=match (k,literal){
                (ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..},Value::String(s))=>s.chars().filter(|c|!c.is_whitespace()).count()>=*min as usize&&s.chars().count()<=*max as usize,
                (ir::ArgType::Int,Value::Int(_))=>true,
                (ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>(*min..=*max).contains(n),_=>false,
            };
            if valid&&!vals.contains(literal){vals.insert(0,literal.clone());}
        }
        let mut next=Vec::new();
        'product:for offset in 0..vals.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(vals.len()){
            if out.exhausted(){return next;}
            let mut case=cases[n%cases.len()].clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[(n+offset)%vals.len()].clone());
            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break 'product;}
        }}
        cases=next;
    }
    let focused=super::executed::write_arguments(bound,p).into_iter().filter(|a|matches!(p.args.get(a),Some(ir::ArgType::Optional(_)))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if focused.is_empty(){return cases;}
    let mut prioritized=Vec::new();
    for case in cases{
        if !prioritized.contains(&case){prioritized.push(case.clone());}
        for name in &focused{if let Some(value)=arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.clone(),p.args[name].clone())]),4).and_then(|mut a|a.remove(name))&&value!=Value::Null{
            let mut alternate=case.clone();alternate.insert(name.clone(),value);if !prioritized.contains(&alternate){prioritized.push(alternate);}
        }}
        if prioritized.len()>=LIMIT{prioritized.truncate(LIMIT);out.unsupported.insert("column-write argument frontier capped at 512; remaining combinations uncovered".into());break;}
    }prioritized
}
pub(super) fn trace_key(steps:&[Step])->Vec<(&str,&str)>{steps.iter().map(|s|(s.operation.as_str(),s.viewer.as_str())).collect()}
pub(super) fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str)->bool{
    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){return false;}
    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});true
}
pub(super) fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value)->bool{
    typed_disclosure_image(ty,data,table,key,column,physical,None)
}
pub(super) fn typed_disclosure_image(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value,policy:Option<&RuleExpr>)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    let payload=|data:&Value|matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key)&&column.is_none_or(|c|data.field(c).ok().is_some_and(|v|physical.field(c).ok()==Some(v)))&&policy.is_none_or(|p|!p.any_term(&|term|{
        let column=match term{RuleTerm::Field(c)|RuleTerm::OldField(c)|RuleTerm::NextField(c)=>Some(c.as_str()),RuleTerm::Reference{column,..}|RuleTerm::ReferenceChain{column,..}=>Some(column.as_str()),_=>None};
        column.filter(|c|*c!="$security:server-clock").is_some_and(|c|data.field(c).is_err()||data.field(c).ok()!=physical.field(c).ok())
    }));
    match ty{
        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>payload(data),
        Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::Omittable(inner)=>typed_disclosure_image(inner,data,table,key,column,physical,policy),
        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(payload)),
        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure_image(inner,r,table,key,column,physical,policy))),
        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure_image(ty,v,table,key,column,physical,policy))),
        _=>false,
    }
}
pub(super) fn typed_source(ty:&crate::types::Ty,table:&str)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    match ty{Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t)|Ty::Page(t,_)=>t==table,Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::Omittable(inner)|Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>typed_source(inner,table),Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.values().any(|ty|typed_source(ty,table)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
    loop{let before=out.len();
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||super::super::columns::rules(t).any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
        if out.len()==before{return out;}
    }
}
fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
    if !out.reserve(calls){return false;}
    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
}
fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
        }else{true}
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if queries.is_empty(){return;}
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true));
        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|super::super::columns::rules(t).any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}

        let observing=observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
            if out.exhausted(){return;}
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){continue;}
            if !reserve_observers(out,1){return;}
            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
            if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].written_by.get(c)).is_some_and(|gate|!gate.audience&&row.field(&gate.owner).ok()!=Some(&Value::Id(viewer.clone()))){continue 'actors;}

            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|typed_source(&p.result,&inv.table)){
                let cases=fixture_cases(bound,&bp.program,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&bp.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",bp.program.name));continue;}};
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){
                        out.attempted+=1;
                        if !matches!(e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)),Ok(again) if !again.unavailable&&again.data==q.data){
                            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read payload is not repeatable on the committed image",bp.program.name));continue;
                        }
                        let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,if inv.column.is_some(){"column read observation"}else{"read"});
                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){
                        out.attempted+=1;
                        if !matches!(e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)),Ok(again) if !again.unavailable&&again.data==q.data){
                            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read payload is not repeatable on the committed image",bp.program.name));continue;
                        }
                        let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,if inv.column.is_some(){"column read observation"}else{"read"});
                        if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query repeatably returned a compiler-typed row/column payload of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
                        continue 'actors;
                    }
                }
            }
            if inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue 'actors;}
            if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history paired absence needs a referentially valid closure",inv.table));continue;}
            let Ok(id)=key.as_str()else{continue;};
            if !reserve_observers(out,3){return;}
            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world invalid: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row,&mut absent)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|observing.iter().any(|t|super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t))){
                let p=&bp.program;
                let cases=fixture_cases(bound,p,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,4){return;}
                    let raw_args=args;
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&p.args,&raw_args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
                    if args!=raw_args{
                        if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                        absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger fork: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
                        if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger absence: {why}",p.name));continue;}
                        if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: ledger absence world invalid: {why}",p.name));continue;}
                        if readback(&absent)!=Ok(absent_rows.clone()){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired argument ledger changes the absent base world",p.name));continue;}
                    }
                    out.attempted+=2;
                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
                    if a==b{continue;}
                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: paired history observation is unstable or unavailable on repeat",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;}
                    let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
                    if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
                    continue 'actors;
                }
            }
        }}
    }
}
fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
}
fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
            }
        }
    }
}
fn changed(bound:&Bound,_p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
    // Disappearance is coverage evidence only. Actual delete authority is
    // judged by the executed-write trace, never by a commit-end image diff.
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|matches!(i.target.as_str(),"delete"|"write")){
        if bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].is_expiring()&&before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|new|new.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: implicit retirement is not an executed delete; delete intent requires an authored write boundary",inv.target_label()));
        }
    }
}

fn role_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut groups=Vec::new();
    let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
    if !ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(table,_)|!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table).is_empty()){return out;}
    if !campaign.reserve(1){return Vec::new();}
        (ir::ArgType::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(e,viewer,types)?,
        _=>v.clone(),
    })}
    values.iter().map(|(n,v)|one(engine,&args[n],v,viewer).map(|v|(n.clone(),v))).collect()
}

/// Fixture-free campaigns retain empty-start bootstraps and populated role worlds.
/// The two initial states share the existing 512-single/512-pair work envelope.
pub fn generated_campaign(bound:&Bound,campaign:&mut Campaign,matrix:bool){
    for (name,_) in bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(_,t)|t.ephemeral.is_some()){campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("ephemeral {name}: generated memory populations and ephemeral mutation histories remain uncovered; durable campaigns use isolated hub copies"));}
    campaign.history_limit=64;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
    if !campaign.reserve(1){return;}
    match engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new()){
        Ok(empty)=>fixture_observers(bound,&empty,campaign),
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("empty initial campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    campaign.history_limit=448;campaign.generated_fixture=true;
    match generated_fixture(bound,campaign){
        Ok(fixture)=>if matrix{operations(bound,&fixture,campaign)}else{fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,campaign)},
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    campaign.history_limit=0;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
}
/// Exercise every authored query/mutation with cross-principal candidates.
/// This is conformance exploration, not a guessed spec oracle: only declared
/// policies and replayed violations can fail security acceptance.
pub fn fixture_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    let first=campaign.violations.len();
    column_reads::direct_fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
    histories::initial_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    histories::lifecycle_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    column_reads::fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
    if !campaign.generated_fixture {trusted_reads(bound,fixture,campaign);}
    histories::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    if campaign.generated_fixture {for witness in &mut campaign.violations[first..] {
        if witness.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace {witness.reachability=Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace;witness.scope="actual authored observation from a validated generated initial fixture; fixture initialization is not claimed application-reachable".into();}
    }}
}

// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#143-concrete-role-refutations-and-coupled-worlds — failed implication only orders a concrete, authored replay.
pub(super) fn direct_refutations(bound:&Bound,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    let first=campaign.violations.len();
    campaign.generated_fixture=true;
    match generated_fixture(bound,campaign){
        Ok(fixture)=>{
            column_reads::direct_fixture(bound,&fixture,campaign);
            histories::initial_observers(bound,&fixture,campaign);
        },
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("direct read witness unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    for v in &mut campaign.violations[first..]{
        if v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace{
            v.reachability=Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace;
            v.scope="actual authored observation from an insert-admitted generated fixture; initialization is not claimed reachable from an empty application".into();
        }
    }
    campaign.generated_fixture=false;
}
pub fn operations(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    let first=campaign.violations.len();
    fixture_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    assets::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    let fixtures=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    let has_fixture=fixtures.values().any(|r|!r.is_empty());
    if fixtures.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{campaign.unsupported.insert("operation fixture matrix exceeds 1000 rows; provide a focused security fixture".into());return;}
    let mut viewers=histories::viewers(bound);
//! Creation is a write even when an edit operation has the stronger guard.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#151-every-executed-write — conditional advice never infers creation policy.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let documents=sources.iter().map(|(file,text)|crate::parse_document(file,text).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let catalog=Catalog::build(documents.clone(),&mut Vec::new());
    let mut writes=Vec::new();
    for doc in &documents{for p in &doc.programs{if p.kind!=ast::ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}
        for (write,context) in super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p){
            let ast::Expr::Write{op,table,fields,site,..}=write else{continue;};
            let Some(info)=catalog.tables.get(&table)else{continue;};
            let requirements=super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,&context);
            let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
            let gate=if op==ast::WriteOp::Update{t.update_rule_judged().into_owned()}else{t.rules.insert.clone()};
            let admission=RuleExpr::And(requirements.into_iter().chain([gate]).collect());
            writes.push((p.name.clone(),op,table,fields,site,admission));
        }
    }}
    fn necessary(rule:&RuleExpr)->RuleExpr{match rule{
        RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(necessary).collect()),
        _ if super::operation::safe(rule)=>rule.clone(),_=>RuleExpr::Allow,
    }}
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
    for (name,op,table,fields,site,admission) in &writes{
        if !matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert){continue;}
        for (editor,edit,other,patch,_,restricted) in &writes{
            if *edit!=ast::WriteOp::Update||other!=table{continue;}
            let restriction=necessary(restricted);
            if kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,&restriction)==Ok(true)||kernel::implies(admission,&restriction)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            let columns=patch.iter().filter(|f|fields.iter().any(|insert|{
                if insert.name!=f.name{return false;}
                let Some(value)=&insert.value else{return true;};
                let mut value=value.clone();let mut supplied=false;
                super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value,&mut |e|if let ast::Expr::Name{name:argument,..}=e{
                    supplied|=documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.programs).filter(|p|p.name==*name).any(|p|p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==*argument));
                });supplied
            })).map(|f|f.name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
            if columns.is_empty()||!seen.insert((name.clone(),table.clone(),columns.clone(),restriction.quarry())){continue;}
            out.push(Diagnostic::new(site,"N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT",format!("{name}: {table}.{} can be supplied at insert/upsert without the edit restriction of {editor}: {}; creation admission: {}",columns.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(","),restriction.quarry(),admission.quarry()),"confirm whether the spec permits different creation authority; otherwise guard every insert/upsert arm too and declare column write only intent, then run snapback4 test").guide("security"));
        }
    }out
}

-- Wash Fold Laundry — Snapback 4 (Quarry) schema, v1.
-- Roles: customer, attendant, driver, owner (roster in `roles`).
-- Operators bootstrap via the service:operator seat; owners manage staff after that.

use identity password

table roles:
  principal: principal
  role: enum('customer', 'attendant', 'driver', 'owner')
  unique principal
  immutable principal
  read <- .principal = viewer or exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] or viewer = 'service:operator'
  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  update <- viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  delete <- viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  online only

table driverRoutes:
  driver: principal
  route: text 1..40
  unique driver
  immutable driver
  read <- .driver = viewer or exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] or viewer = 'service:operator'
  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  update <- viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  delete <- viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  online only

-- One bag drop-off. priceCents is computed server-side from weight (25c/oz).
table orders:
  customer: principal
  weightOz: int 1..3200
  priceCents: int 0..1000000
  tagPhoto: image <=5000000
  status: enum('washing', 'drying', 'folding', 'ready', 'pickedup', 'donated') default 'washing'
  route: text 1..40
  droppedAt: time default now
  readyAt: time ?
  pickedUpAt: time ?
  immutable customer, weightOz, droppedAt
  read <- .customer = viewer or exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists driverRoutes[viewer, route = .route]
  insert <- exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  update <- exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] or .customer = viewer
  online only

-- A customer's lost/damaged claim; only the owner decides it.
table claims:
  order: orders
  customer: principal
  reason: enum('lost', 'damaged')
  detail: text 1..500
  status: enum('pending', 'approved', 'denied') default 'pending'
  refundCents: int ?
  at: time default now
  unique order else ALREADY_CLAIMED 'This order already has a claim.'
  immutable order, customer, reason, detail, at
  read <- .customer = viewer or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  insert <- .customer = viewer
  update <- exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  online only

query myRole():
  return roles[viewer]

query allRoles(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OWNER
  return roles first 50 by .id after c

query myOrders(c: cursor ?):
  return orders where .customer = viewer last 50 by .droppedAt after c { *, overdue: .droppedAt + 2d < now and (.status = 'washing' or .status = 'drying' or .status = 'folding') }

  return orders[order]

  return orders where .customer = viewer and (.status = 'ready' or .status = 'pickedup') last 50 by .droppedAt after c

  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_STAFF

  return driverRoutes[viewer]

  return claims where .customer = viewer last 50 by .at after c

query allClaims(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OWNER
  return claims last 50 by .at after c

mutation provision(principal: principal, role: enum('customer', 'attendant', 'driver', 'owner')):
  require viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OPERATOR
  return upsert roles[principal] { principal, role }

mutation assignRoute(driver: principal, route: text 1..40):
  require viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OWNER
  require exists roles[driver, role = 'driver'] else NOT_DRIVER
  return upsert driverRoutes[driver] { driver, route }

mutation dropOff(customer: principal, weightOz: int 1..3200, tagPhoto: image <=5000000, route: text 1..40):
  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_ATTENDANT
  require exists roles[customer, role = 'customer'] else NOT_CUSTOMER 'Drop-offs are logged for customer accounts.'
  return insert orders { customer, weightOz, priceCents: weightOz * 25, tagPhoto, route }

mutation advanceStatus(order: orders, status: enum('drying', 'folding', 'ready')):
  o = orders[order] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_STAFF
  require (o.status = 'washing' and status = 'drying') or (o.status = 'drying' and status = 'folding') or (o.status = 'folding' and status = 'ready') else BAD_TRANSITION 'Bags move washing -> drying -> folding -> ready.'
  update orders[order] { status, readyAt: now if status = 'ready' else o.readyAt }
  return null

mutation pickup(order: orders):
  o = orders[order] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require o.customer = viewer or exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_YOURS
  require o.status = 'ready' else NOT_READY 'This bag is not ready for pickup.'
  require (o.readyAt ?? 0) > now - 30d else TOO_LATE 'Unclaimed bags are donated after 30 days.'
  update orders[order] { status: 'pickedup', pickedUpAt: now }
  return null

mutation fileClaim(order: orders, reason: enum('lost', 'damaged'), detail: text 1..500):
  o = orders[order] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require o.customer = viewer else NOT_YOURS 'Claims are filed by the order owner.'
  return insert claims { order, customer: viewer, reason, detail }

mutation decideClaim(claim: claims, approve: bool, refundCents: int ?):
  c = claims[claim] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OWNER 'Only the store owner approves refunds.'
  require c.status = 'pending' else ALREADY_DECIDED 'This claim was already decided.'
  require not approve or (refundCents ?? 0) > 0 else BAD_REFUND 'An approved refund needs an amount.'
  require approve or refundCents = null else BAD_REFUND 'A denied claim carries no amount.'
  update claims[claim] { status: 'approved' if approve else 'denied', refundCents }
  return null

job donateUnclaimed(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time):
  for o in orders by .id:
    if o.status = 'ready' and (o.readyAt ?? 0) < periodEnd - 30d:
      update orders[o.id] { status: 'donated' }

schedule daily every 1d at 06:00: donateUnclaimed
//! Advisory lineage through compiled result/refusal expressions, never a proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#152-mutation-results-are-observations — projection is one observer, not a privacy boundary.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::ir::{self,Expr,Stmt};
type Sources=BTreeSet<(String,String)>;
#[derive(Clone,Default)]
struct Flow{sources:Sources,fields:BTreeMap<String,Flow>}
impl Flow{
    fn all(&self)->Sources{self.sources.iter().cloned().chain(self.fields.values().flat_map(Flow::all)).collect()}
    fn union(xs:impl IntoIterator<Item=Flow>)->Self{Self{sources:xs.into_iter().flat_map(|f|f.all()).collect(),fields:BTreeMap::new()}}
    fn join(mut a:Flow,b:Flow)->Flow{a.sources.extend(b.sources);for(n,v)in b.fields{let old=a.fields.remove(&n).unwrap_or_default();a.fields.insert(n,Self::join(old,v));}a}
}
fn row(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->Flow{Flow{sources:Sources::new(),fields:bound.schema.tables.get(table).map(|t|t.columns.keys().map(|c|(c.clone(),Flow{sources:BTreeSet::from([(table.into(),c.clone())]),fields:BTreeMap::new()})).collect()).unwrap_or_default()}}
fn value(bound:&Bound,e:&Expr,env:&mut BTreeMap<String,Flow>,reads:&mut BTreeSet<String>,returned:&mut Sources,refusals:&mut Sources)->Flow{match e{
    Expr::Variable(n)=>env.get(n).cloned().unwrap_or_default(),
    Expr::Field{base,name}=>{let f=value(bound,base,env,reads,returned,refusals);f.fields.get(name).cloned().unwrap_or(Flow{sources:f.sources,fields:BTreeMap::new()})},
    Expr::Object(fields)=>Flow{sources:Sources::new(),fields:fields.iter().map(|(n,v)|(n.clone(),value(bound,v,env,reads,returned,refusals))).collect()},
    Expr::Shape{base,fields}=>{let _=value(bound,base,env,reads,returned,refusals);Flow{sources:Sources::new(),fields:fields.iter().map(|(n,v)|(n.clone(),value(bound,v,env,reads,returned,refusals))).collect()}},
    Expr::Get(r)=>{reads.insert(r.table.clone());row(bound,&r.table)},
    Expr::Scan(s)=>{reads.insert(s.table.clone());row(bound,&s.table)},
    Expr::Merge(m)=>{reads.insert(m.table().into());row(bound,m.table())},
    Expr::Array(xs)|Expr::Builtin{args:xs,..}=>{
        // ProjectFields is the engine's column gate, retaining row identity.
        if let Expr::Builtin{builtin:ir::Builtin::ProjectFields{table},..}=e{
            reads.insert(table.clone());let input=Flow::union(xs.iter().map(|e|value(bound,e,env,reads,returned,refusals)));return Flow::join(input,row(bound,table));
        }
        if let Expr::Builtin{builtin:ir::Builtin::OptionalField{field,..},..}=e{
            let f=value(bound,&xs[0],env,reads,returned,refusals);return f.fields.get(field).cloned().unwrap_or(Flow{sources:f.sources,fields:BTreeMap::new()});
        }
        if matches!(e,Expr::Builtin{builtin:ir::Builtin::ProjectFields{..}|ir::Builtin::RequireUncapped|ir::Builtin::First|ir::Builtin::Last|ir::Builtin::At|ir::Builtin::Sort{..},..})&&!xs.is_empty(){return value(bound,&xs[0],env,reads,returned,refusals);}
        Flow::union(xs.iter().map(|e|value(bound,e,env,reads,returned,refusals)))
    },
    Expr::Binary{left,right,..}=>{
        let a=value(bound,left,env,reads,returned,refusals);let b=value(bound,right,env,reads,returned,refusals);
        if matches!(&**left,Expr::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null))&&!b.fields.is_empty()||matches!(&**right,Expr::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null))&&!a.fields.is_empty(){Flow::default()}else{Flow::union([a,b])}
    },
    Expr::Unary{value:e,..}=>value(bound,e,env,reads,returned,refusals),
    Expr::Block{body,value:e}=>{statements(bound,body,env,reads,returned,refusals);value(bound,e,env,reads,returned,refusals)},
    _=>Flow::default(),
}}
fn statements(bound:&Bound,body:&[Stmt],env:&mut BTreeMap<String,Flow>,reads:&mut BTreeSet<String>,returned:&mut Sources,refusals:&mut Sources){for s in body{match s{
    Stmt::Const{name,value:e}|Stmt::Let{name,value:e}|Stmt::Assign{name,value:e}=>{let f=value(bound,e,env,reads,returned,refusals);env.insert(name.clone(),f);},
    Stmt::Push{target,value:e}=>{let f=value(bound,e,env,reads,returned,refusals);let old=env.remove(target).unwrap_or_default();env.insert(target.clone(),Flow::union([old,f]));},
    Stmt::Insert{table,row:Expr::Variable(n)}|Stmt::InsertIfAbsent{table,row:Expr::Variable(n),..}=>{let prior=env.remove(n).unwrap_or_default();env.insert(n.clone(),Flow::join(prior,row(bound,table)));},
    Stmt::WriteTarget{target,result:Some(n),..}=>{reads.insert(target.table.clone());env.insert(n.clone(),row(bound,&target.table));},
    Stmt::Return(e)=>{returned.extend(value(bound,e,env,reads,&mut Sources::new(),refusals).all());},
    Stmt::Require{condition,..}|Stmt::Check{condition,..}=>{refusals.extend(value(bound,condition,env,reads,returned,&mut Sources::new()).all());},
    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body}=>{
        let condition=value(bound,condition,env,reads,returned,refusals).all();
        // A private branch can choose both results and authored refusals.
        returned.extend(condition.iter().cloned());refusals.extend(condition);
        let mut other=env.clone();statements(bound,then_body,env,reads,returned,refusals);statements(bound,else_body,&mut other,reads,returned,refusals);
        for (n,f) in other{let old=env.remove(&n).unwrap_or_default();env.insert(n,Flow::join(old,f));}
    },
    Stmt::For{binding,domain,body}=>{match domain{
        ir::LoopDomain::Page(s)=>{reads.insert(s.table.clone());env.insert(binding.clone(),row(bound,&s.table));},
        ir::LoopDomain::DerivedArray{value:e,..}=>{let f=value(bound,e,env,reads,returned,refusals);env.insert(binding.clone(),f);},_=>{},
    }statements(bound,body,env,reads,returned,refusals);},_=>{},
}}}
pub(super) fn notes(bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let flows=bound.programs.iter().map(|bp|{let(mut reads,mut returned,mut refusals)=(BTreeSet::new(),Sources::new(),Sources::new());statements(bound,&bp.program.body,&mut BTreeMap::new(),&mut reads,&mut returned,&mut refusals);(bp,reads,returned,refusals)}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut hidden:BTreeMap<(String,String),BTreeSet<String>>=BTreeMap::new();
    for (bp,reads,returned,_) in &flows{if bp.program.kind!=ir::ProgramKind::Query{continue;}
        for table in reads{if let Some(t)=bound.schema.tables.get(table){for c in t.columns.keys().filter(|c|c.as_str()!="id"){
            if !returned.contains(&(table.clone(),c.clone())){hidden.entry((table.clone(),c.clone())).or_default().insert(bp.program.name.clone());}
        }}}
    }
    let mut grouped=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<(Site,String)>>::new();
    for (bp,_,returned,refusals) in flows{
        for (kind,origins) in [("return/write result",returned),("refusal/branch",refusals)]{
            let mut groups:BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>>=BTreeMap::new();
            for (table,c) in origins{
                let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
                let projected=hidden.get(&(table.clone(),c.clone()));
                let ruled=t.column_reads.contains_key(&c)||t.written_by.get(&c).is_some_and(|g|!g.audience);
                if projected.is_none()&&!ruled{continue;}
                // A query's own narrowed result is not an alternate write observer.
                if bp.program.kind==ir::ProgramKind::Query&&kind=="return/write result"&&projected.is_none(){continue;}
                let reason=projected.map(|qs|format!("omitted by {}",qs.iter().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))).unwrap_or_else(||"column-ruled".into());
                groups.entry(table).or_default().push(format!("{c} ({reason})"));
            }
            for (table,columns) in groups{grouped.entry(table).or_default().push((bp.site.clone(),format!("{} at {}: {kind} observes {}",bp.program.name,bp.site,columns.join(", "))));}
        }
    }
    grouped.into_iter().map(|(table,mut paths)|{
        paths.sort_by_key(|(site,_)|(site.file.clone(),site.line,site.col));
        Diagnostic::new(&paths[0].0,"N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE",format!("{table}: {}; row audience: {}",paths.iter().map(|(_,text)|text.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; "),bound.schema.tables[&table].rules.read.quarry()),"confirm each operation's result/refusal audience from the spec; a query projection alone is not privacy. Use column read rules and independent intent; run snapback4 test").guide("security")
    }).collect()
}
//! Repairs lead; uncertainty stays visible without repeating every proof blocker.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#144-actions-precede-coverage-details — advice is separate from replayed failures.
use super::{Report, Status};

/// One consequence ordering shared by check and audit. Lower means more urgent.
pub fn note_priority(code: &str) -> u8 {
    match code {
        "N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT" | "N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE" => 0,
        "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION" | "N_SELF_CLAIMED_RELATIONSHIP" => 1,
        "N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE" | "N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" |
        "N_BROAD_READ" | "N_CALLER_TIME" | "N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE" |
        "N_CREATION_REASON" | "N_CREATION_MONEY" | "N_CONSTANT_APPROVAL" |
        "N_TRANSITION_BYPASS" | "N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK" | "N_DURATION_BOUNDS" |
        "N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE" => 2,
        "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE" | "N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT" => 5,
        "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" => 6,
        "N_SECURITY_INTENT" => 7,
        _ => 4,
    }
}

impl Report {
    /// Full structured evidence remains in the report; this is a human action list.
    pub fn action_text(&self, limit: usize) -> String {
        let failures = self.invariants.iter().filter(|v| v.status == Status::Refuted &&
            v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c| c.reachability.is_reachable())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let mut notes = self.notes.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        notes.sort_by_key(|n| (note_priority(&n.code), n.site.file.clone(), n.site.line, n.site.col, &n.code, &n.message));
        notes.dedup_by(|a,b| a.site == b.site && a.code == b.code && a.message == b.message);
        let mut out = format!("security actions: {} fix{}, {} review{}\n", failures.len(),
            if failures.len() == 1 { "" } else { "es" }, notes.len(), if notes.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" });
        for v in &failures {
            let c = v.counterexample.as_ref().unwrap();
            out.push_str(&format!("  FIX {}: {} via {} as {} ({}); tighten enforcement to the declared intent, then run snapback4 test\n",
                v.invariant.site, v.invariant.target_label(), c.operation, c.viewer, serde_json::to_value(&c.reachability).unwrap().as_str().unwrap()));
        }
        let mut shown = 0;
        for note in notes.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(i,n)|
            (i < limit || note_priority(&n.code) <= 2).then_some(*n)) {
            shown += 1;
            out.push_str(&format!("  REVIEW {} {}: {}\n    {}\n",note.site,note.code,note.message,note.rewrite));
        }
        if shown < notes.len() { out.push_str(&format!("  {} more reviews: snapback4 audit --all or check --verbose\n", notes.len()-shown)); }
        let proved = self.invariants.iter().filter(|v| v.status == Status::Proved).count();
        let refuted = self.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==Status::Refuted).count();
        let unproved = self.invariants.len()-proved-refuted;
        out.push_str(&format!("security: {proved} proved, {refuted} refuted, {unproved} unproved ({} failing)\n", failures.len()));
        let supplemental = self.invariants.iter().filter(|v| v.status == Status::Refuted &&
            v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c| !c.reachability.is_reachable())).count();
        if supplemental > 0 {out.push_str(&format!("  {supplemental} seed/synthetic observations are non-failing\n"));}
        if unproved > 0 || !self.campaign_coverage.is_empty() {
            out.push_str(&format!("  next: snapback4 test; {} coverage gaps (audit --all/JSON keeps every reason)\n",self.campaign_coverage.len()));
        }
        if self.invariants.is_empty() {out.push_str("  next: answer snapback/generated/security-invariants.q.draft from the spec; declare intent and run snapback4 test\n");}
        out
    }
}

//! Narrow stable leading-require reduction for operation-specific authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown write sets never establish stability.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Stmt,ProgramDecl,ProgramKind,InvariantDecl,RuleDecl};
use super::super::catalog::TableInfo;
pub(super) fn resolve<'a>(catalog:&'a Catalog,decl:&InvariantDecl)->Option<(String,&'a ProgramDecl)>{
    if matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"always"|"write"){return None;}
    let matches=catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|{let module=std::path::Path::new(&d.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();d.programs.iter().map(move|p|(super::super::operation_name(&module,&p.name),p))})
        .filter(|(name,p)|p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(name==&decl.target||p.name==decl.target)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if matches.len()==1 {matches.into_iter().next()}else{None}
}
pub(super) fn safe(rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {match rule{
    RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}=>key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().all(safe),
    RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>[a,b].iter().all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Deny|RuleExpr::Public{..}=>true,_=>false,
}}
pub(super) fn requirements(catalog:&Catalog,info:&TableInfo,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<RuleExpr>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body){let Stmt::Require{condition,site,..}=s else{break};
        let decl=RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:condition.clone(),site:site.clone()};
        if let Ok(rule)=super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&decl)&&safe(&rule){out.push(rule);}
    }out
}
pub(super) fn prove(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<bool,String>{
    let p=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|Some(&p.program.name)==inv.operation.as_ref()).ok_or("operation did not bind")?;
    if !safe(inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("unsupported policy")?){return Err("operation policy needs unsupported target-image, negative-probe or live-reference bindings".into());}
    let writes=snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program);
    if !writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table){return Err("operation has no direct write to the named table; implicit effects require a reduction".into());}
    let dependencies=inv.requirements.iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|dependencies.contains(t)) {return Err("require dependency can be changed by this operation, including its target write".into());}
    if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.maintained.is_empty()||!t.sequences.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())) || !bound.schema.maintains.is_empty(){return Err("implicit maintain/sequence/referential writes require dependency stability reduction".into());}
    // Source helpers/control flow may hide write sets or viewer changes.
    let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool{match v{serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"For"|"If"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"|"Emit"))||m.values().any(opaque),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false}}
    if opaque(&json){return Err("operation control flow or opaque effects are outside stable leading-require reduction".into());}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(inv.requirements.clone());
    // Table-rule gates can only further restrict a dominating stable require;
    // using the require alone overapproximates effective owner-field gates.
    kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())
}
    }

    pub fn store_path(&self) -> PathBuf {
        self.data_dir().join("store.sqlite")
    }

    /// Every `.q` file under `snapback/`, `schema.q` first, as `(name, text)`.
    pub fn sources(&self) -> std::io::Result<Vec<(String, String)>> {
        let mut names: Vec<String> = std::fs::read_dir(self.schema_dir())?
            .flatten()
            .filter_map(|entry| {
                let name = entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_string();
                (name.ends_with(".q") && entry.path().is_file()).then_some(name)
            })
            .collect();
        names.sort();
        if let Some(position) = names.iter().position(|n| n == "schema.q") {
            let schema = names.remove(position);
            names.insert(0, schema);
        }
        names.into_iter().map(|name| Ok((name.clone(), std::fs::read_to_string(self.schema_dir().join(&name))?))).collect()
    }

    pub fn saved_copy_hint(&self) -> Option<String> {
        let sources = self.sources().ok()?;
        let tables: Vec<_> = sources.iter().map(|(file, text)| (file, snapback4_lang::parse_document(file, text).0.tables.into_iter().map(|t| t.name).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>())).collect();
        for (i, (file, names)) in tables.iter().enumerate() {
            if names.is_empty() { continue; }
            for (other, next) in &tables[i + 1..] {
                if names == next { return Some(format!("{file} and {other} declare the same tables — a saved copy? keep saved sources outside snapback/")); }
            }
        }
        None
    }

    /// the native crate's manifest and `src/` — for the watcher.
    pub fn sources_stamp(&self) -> SourceStamp {
        fn newest(dir: &Path, keep: &dyn Fn(&Path) -> bool, depth: u8) -> u128 {
            std::fs::read_dir(dir)
                .map(|entries| {
//! Diagnostics: one envelope, located, with a rewrite when a safe repair is known
//! and the keyword card to read (LLP 2000.000 §5.1).

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::fmt;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Site {
    pub file: String,
    pub line: u32,
    pub col: u32,
}

impl Site {
    pub fn new(file: &str, line: u32, col: u32) -> Self {
        Self { file: file.to_owned(), line, col }
    }
}

impl fmt::Display for Site {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}:{}:{}", self.file, self.line, self.col)
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum SourceRewriteKind {
    MixedKey,
    NamedUpdate,
    WriteResultNarrowing,
    MembershipBootstrap,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Diagnostic {
    pub site: Site,
    pub code: String,
    pub message: String,
    pub rewrite: String,
    /// Reservation evidence retained for JSON and check --verbose.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub details: Option<String>,
    /// Exact Quarry source that may replace the diagnostic's authored
    /// expression or rule. Most rewrites are design guidance and leave this
    /// unset; golden tests compile only this compiler-emitted source.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub source_rewrite: Option<String>,
    /// Exhaustive category used by the rewrite golden. Adding a new concrete
    /// rule edit requires adding a category and a compile/semantics case.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub source_rewrite_kind: Option<SourceRewriteKind>,
    pub guide: String,
    /// An opt-in `check --fix` edit: spelling repairs or a verified positional-key choice.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub mechanical: bool,
    /// Parser-proven consequence of an earlier error in this operation,
    /// with no independent information. Kept for verbose and JSON reports.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub cascade_of: Option<Site>,
    /// Compiler-only candidate; source is withheld until contextual verification.
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub(crate) read_repair: Option<crate::repair::ReadRepair>,
    /// Exact original span proved by the compiler, never part of wire diagnostics.
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub(crate) verified_edit: Option<(Site, String)>,
}

impl Diagnostic {
    pub fn new(site: &Site, code: &str, message: impl Into<String>, rewrite: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        let code = code.to_owned();
        let guide = guide_for(&code).to_owned();
        Self { site: site.clone(), code, message: message.into(), rewrite: rewrite.into(), details: None, source_rewrite: None, source_rewrite_kind: None, guide, mechanical: false, cascade_of: None, read_repair: None, verified_edit: None }
    }

    pub fn details(mut self, details: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        self.details = Some(details.into());
        self
    }

    /// The CLI must match this original source before applying `rewrite`.
    pub fn verified_edit(&self) -> Option<(&Site, &str)> {
        self.verified_edit.as_ref().map(|(site, source)| (site, source.as_str()))
    }

//! Explicit, shareable review of compiler notes; errors and dynamic notes stay live.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#132-reviewed-notes — source edits invalidate review, and JSON retains every finding.
use crate::project::Project;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use snapback4_lang::Diagnostic;
use std::{collections::BTreeSet, fs, io};

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct File {
    version: u32,
    sources: String,
    reviewed: BTreeSet<String>,
}
pub(super) struct Review {
    pub new: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    pub acknowledged: Vec<Diagnostic>,
}
pub(super) fn fingerprint(note: &Diagnostic) -> String {
    hash(&serde_json::to_vec(note).expect("serializable diagnostic"))
}
fn hash(bytes: &[u8]) -> String { format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(bytes)) }
fn digest(value: &str) -> bool { value.len() == 64 && value.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_hexdigit()) }

pub(super) fn review(project: &Project, compiled_sources: &[(String, String)], notes: &[Diagnostic], eligible: &BTreeSet<String>, acknowledge: bool) -> Result<Review, String> {
    let path = project.schema_dir().join("check-notes.json");
    // Persist the input actually compiled, never a later editor save paired
    // with old diagnostics. A later edit will also invalidate this digest.
    if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? != compiled_sources {
        return Err("Quarry sources changed during check; rerun before reviewing notes".into());
    }
    let mut sources = compiled_sources.to_vec();
    sources.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
    let sources = hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&sources).expect("serializable sources"));
    let failure = |error: String| format!("cannot read {}: {error}; inspect and remove or repair this file, then rerun check --ack-notes", path.display());
    let previous: Option<File> = match fs::read(&path) {
        Ok(bytes) => {
            let file: File = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|e| failure(e.to_string()))?;
            if file.version != 1 || !digest(&file.sources) || file.reviewed.iter().any(|s| !digest(s)) {
                return Err(failure("unsupported format (expected version 1 and SHA-256 digests)".into()));
            }
            Some(file)
        }
        Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => None,
        Err(e) => return Err(failure(e.to_string())),
    };
    let current: BTreeSet<_> = notes.iter().map(fingerprint).filter(|f| eligible.contains(f)).collect();
    let reviewed = if acknowledge {
        let file = File { version: 1, sources: sources.clone(), reviewed: current.clone() };
        let temporary = path.with_extension(format!("json.{}.tmp", std::process::id()));
        let bytes = serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&file).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        // Rename publishes a complete file; a failed write preserves the old review.
        fs::write(&temporary, bytes).and_then(|_| fs::rename(&temporary, &path)).map_err(|e| {
            let _ = fs::remove_file(&temporary);
            format!("cannot write {}: {e}", path.display())
        })?;
        current
    } else {
        previous.filter(|file| file.sources == sources).map(|file| file.reviewed).unwrap_or_default()
    };
    let (acknowledged, new) = notes.iter().cloned().partition(|note| {
        let key = fingerprint(note);
        eligible.contains(&key) && reviewed.contains(&key)
    });
    Ok(Review { new, acknowledged })
}

            return self.write_inner(&resolved, out, valued);

        }
        for field in fields {
            if info.decl.columns.iter().any(|c| c.name == field.name && c.sequence.is_some()) {

        self.note_denied_write(info, op, fields, site);

        // (including an expanded helper) belongs in a changed-prediction note.
        let write_name = format!("{} {table}", op.word());
        self.sources.insert(format!("write/{write_name}/{site}"), (site.clone(), write_name));
        match op {
            WriteOp::Insert => {
                let (row, row_ty) = self.write_row(info, fields, None, site, out)?;
                let row_ty = if self.schema.tables[table].column_reads.is_empty() || self.ordinary_write_lifetime(table) && self.row_independent_read_proved(table) { row_ty } else { row_ty.optional() };
                let tmp = self.temp("inserted");
                out.push(Stmt::Const { name: tmp.clone(), value: row });
                if *if_absent {
                    let result = self.temp("absent");
                    out.push(Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { table: table.clone(), row: ir::var(&tmp), result: result.clone() });
                    return Ok(valued.then(|| (self.project_insert(info, ir::var(&result)), row_ty.optional())));
                }
                out.push(Stmt::Insert { table: table.clone(), row: ir::var(&tmp) });
                Ok(valued.then(|| (self.project_insert(info, ir::var(&tmp)), row_ty)))
            }
            WriteOp::Update | WriteOp::Upsert => {
                // @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — upsert's insert arm has no old row on which to decide a condition.
                if *op == WriteOp::Upsert && condition.is_some() {
                    return Err(unknown(site, "upsert cannot take a condition", "a condition belongs on update; an upsert inserts or patches"));
                }
                // @ref LLP 3000.007#8 — item 7: target resolution and conditional old images belong to the engine.
                let key = self.write_key(info, keys, site, out).map_err(|error| self.extra_write_test(info, expr, error))?;
                let upsert = *op == WriteOp::Upsert;
                if upsert && fields.iter().any(|f| f.name == "id") {
                    return Err(unknown(site, "upsert cannot spell id; the key names the row", "remove id from the patch; the key names the row"));
                }
                let (patch, _) = self.write_row(info, fields, Some("patch"), site, out)?;
                self.immutable_patch(info, &key, &patch, fields, site, out, upsert, condition.is_some())?;
                let condition = if let Some(condition) = condition {
                    let old = self.temp("condition");
                    let prior = self.focal.replace((old.clone(), Ty::Row(table.clone())));
                    let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
                    let bound = self.expr(condition, &mut setup);
                    self.focal = prior;
                    let (value, ty) = bound?;
                    if !ty.is_bool() {
                        return Err(type_error(condition.site(), format!("update's condition must be bool, got {}", ty.name()), "update t[key] { body } if .body = expected"));
                    }
                    out.nest(&format!("{}.condition", out.len()), &setup.ownership);
                    Some(ir::WriteCondition { old, setup: setup.code, value })
                } else { None };
                let result = valued.then(|| self.temp("written"));
                let target = Read { site: site.to_string(), table: table.clone(), index: key.index, key: key.key, provenance: KeyProvenance::Shape };
                out.push(Stmt::WriteTarget { target, patch, upsert, condition, result: result.clone() });
                let row_ty = self.write_result_type(info);
                let row_ty = if self.ordinary_write_lifetime(table) && self.row_independent_read_proved(table) {
                    row_ty
                } else { row_ty.optional() };
                Ok(result.map(|name| (ir::var(&name), row_ty)))
            }
            WriteOp::Delete => {
                let key = self.write_key(info, keys, site, out).map_err(|error| self.extra_write_test(info, expr, error))?;
                if key.alternate {
                    let location = self.site();
                    self.sources.insert(location.clone(), (site.clone(), format!("delete {table}")));
                    out.push(Stmt::DeleteTarget { target: Read { site: location, table: table.clone(), index: key.index, key: key.key, provenance: KeyProvenance::Shape } });
                } else {
                }
                Ok((Expr::Object(exprs.into_iter().collect()), Ty::Object(types)))
            }
        }
    }

    fn write_row_with_id(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, fields: &[ObjectField], id: Expr, site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        let id = self.snapshot(id, out);
        let mut exprs = vec![("id".to_owned(), id)];
        let mut given = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        for field in fields {
            let Some(scalar) = info.columns.get(&field.name) else {
                return Err(unknown(&field.site, format!("{} has no column {}", info.decl.name, field.name), name_rewrite(&field.name, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str), None)));
            };
            let expected = Ty::from_scalar(scalar);
            let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
            let (e, t) = self.insert_field_value(info, field, &expected, &mut setup)?;
            literal_in_range(info, &field.name, scalar, &e, &field.site)?;
            self.sequence_setup(exprs.iter_mut().map(|(_, e)| e), setup, out);
            if !t.writable_to(&expected) {
                return Err(diagnostics::write_field_type(info, field, &expected, &t));
            }
            let e = if expr::allocates(&e) { self.snapshot(e, out) } else { e };
            if !given.insert(field.name.clone()) {
                return Err(unknown(&field.site, format!("field {} is written twice", field.name), "keep one"));
            }
            exprs.push((field.name.clone(), e));
        }
        // Materialize the same defaults in the insert expression so its returned row is the written image.
        for column in &info.decl.columns {
            if !given.contains(&column.name) && let Some(default) = &column.default {
                if column.written_by.is_some() || self.schema.receipt_assigned(&info.decl.name, &column.name) {
                    // Preserve omission until the engine checks supplied-field authority.
                    given.insert(column.name.clone());
                    continue;
                }
                let default = super::defaults::column(column, default)?;
                let value = match default {
                    snapback4_core::schema::ColumnDefault::Literal(value) => Expr::Literal(value),
                    snapback4_core::schema::ColumnDefault::Now => Expr::Input(Input::Now),
                    snapback4_core::schema::ColumnDefault::Viewer => Expr::Input(Input::Viewer),
                };
                exprs.push((column.name.clone(), value));
                given.insert(column.name.clone());
            }
        }
        let missing: Vec<&str> = info.columns.iter().filter(|(name, scalar)| !scalar.optional && !self.schema.receipt_assigned(&info.decl.name, name) && !info.decl.columns.iter().any(|c| c.name == **name && c.sequence.is_some()) && !super::columns::fields(info).any(|f|f == *name) && !given.contains(*name)).map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect();
        if !missing.is_empty() {
            return Err(type_error(site, format!("insert into {} omits required column{} {}", info.decl.name, if missing.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, missing.join(", ")), format!("insert {} {{ {} }}", info.decl.name, given.iter().cloned().chain(missing.iter().map(|m| format!("{m}: <{}>", info.columns.get(*m).map(print::scalar_type).unwrap_or_default()))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))));
        }
        Ok((Expr::Object(exprs.into_iter().collect()), self.write_result_type(info)))
    }

    fn project_insert(&self, info: &TableInfo, row: Expr) -> Expr {
        if info.decl.columns.iter().any(|column| column.written_by.is_some()) || !self.schema.tables[&info.decl.name].column_reads.is_empty() {
            Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::ProjectFields { table: info.decl.name.clone() }, args: vec![row] }
        } else { row }
    }

    fn write_result_type(&self, info: &TableInfo) -> Ty {
        if super::columns::fields(info).next().is_some() { Ty::WriteRow(info.decl.name.clone()) } else { Ty::Row(info.decl.name.clone()) }
    }

    fn calendar_number(&self, value: &A, depth: u8) -> bool {
//! Validate the complete command before any project, store or host work.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#4-cli-surface-contracts — admission, every synopsis and flag help share this command table.
use std::sync::OnceLock;
#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
struct Flag { name: String, value: String, #[serde(default)] retired: bool }
#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
struct Command { verb: String, positional: String, flags: Vec<Flag>, #[serde(skip)] allowed: String }
fn commands() -> &'static [Command] {
    static TABLE: OnceLock<Vec<Command>> = OnceLock::new();
    TABLE.get_or_init(|| {
        let mut commands: Vec<Command> = serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../../packages/snapback4/bin/cli-commands.json")).expect("embedded command table");
        for command in &mut commands { command.allowed = command.flags.iter().map(|flag|flag.name.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" "); }
        commands
    })
}
fn command(verb: &str) -> Option<&'static Command> { commands().iter().find(|command|command.verb==verb) }
pub(crate) fn allowed(verb: &str) -> &'static str { command(verb).map_or("", |command| command.allowed.as_str()) }

pub fn validate(args: &[String]) -> Result<(), String> {
    let verb = args.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("");
    // Pipe-owned private commands have positional protocols, not public flags.
    if matches!(verb, "--internal-witness-process" | "--internal-worker-watchdog") { return Ok(()); }
    let mut iter = args.iter().skip(1).peekable();
    while let Some(arg) = iter.next() {
        if arg == "--" { break; }
        if arg == "-h" { continue; }
        let Some(flag) = arg.strip_prefix("--") else {
            if arg.starts_with('-') && !arg[1..].starts_with(|c: char| c.is_ascii_digit()) {
                return Err(if verb == "witness" { "E_INPUT: witness has an invalid flag; see guide witness".into() } else { format!("E_INPUT: {verb} takes no {arg}") });
            }
            continue;
        };
        let (name, inline) = flag.split_once('=').map_or((flag, None), |(name, value)| (name, Some(value)));
        if name != "help" && !allowed(verb).split_whitespace().any(|known| known == name) {
            if verb == "rollback" && name == "dry-run" { return Err("E_INPUT: rollback preview is --check; use rollback <dir> [--to N|latest] --check".into()); }
            // Witness arguments may contain bearer material even in a flag name.
            return Err(if verb == "witness" { "E_INPUT: witness has an unknown flag; see guide witness".into() } else { format!("E_INPUT: {verb} takes no --{name}") });
        }
        if switch(name) {
            if inline.is_some() { return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} takes no value")); }
        } else if inline.is_none() {
            // Retired spellings route to their existing no-work explanation,
            // even with a missing value; new unknown flags still refuse here.
            if retired(verb,name) {
                if iter.peek().is_some_and(|value| !value.starts_with("--")) { iter.next(); }
                continue;
            }
            let Some(value) = iter.next() else { return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} needs a value")); };
            // Search text is literal, including leading dashes and punctuation.
            if value.starts_with("--") && !matches!(name, "find" | "section") {
                return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} needs a value"));
            }
        }
    }
    let has = |name: &str| args.iter().skip(1).any(|arg| arg == &format!("--{name}") || arg.starts_with(&format!("--{name}=")));
    if verb == "check" {
        if has("ack-security")!=has("reason"){return Err("E_INPUT: --ack-security ID requires --reason TEXT".into());}
        if has("ack-security")&&["ack-notes","fix","store","release","emit-lean","audit"].iter().any(|f|has(f)){return Err("E_INPUT: individual security review cannot combine with --ack-notes, --fix, saved-store/release, --emit-lean or --audit".into());}
        if has("release") && !has("store") { return Err("E_INPUT: check --release requires --store <path>".into()); }
        if has("as") && !has("residency") { return Err("E_INPUT: check --as requires --residency".into()); }
        let incompatible = if has("emit-lean") { Some(("--emit-lean", &["cost","residency","fix","verbose","store","fixture","as","release","ack-notes","audit"][..])) }
            else if has("release") { Some(("--store --release", &["cost","residency","fix","fixture","as","ack-notes"][..])) } else { None };
        if let Some((mode,flags)) = incompatible {
            if let Some(flag) = flags.iter().find(|flag|has(flag)) { return Err(format!("E_INPUT: check {mode} does not accept --{flag}")); }
        }
    }
    if verb == "serve" && has("wait-stopped") && args.iter().any(|arg|arg.starts_with("--") && !matches!(arg.as_str(),"--wait-stopped"|"--help")) {
        return Err("E_INPUT: serve --wait-stopped takes only a deployment directory; it does not start or signal a server".into());
    }
    if verb == "rollback" && has("bridge") && !has("check") { return Err("E_INPUT: rollback --bridge requires --check; it writes a bridge starter without activating it".into()); }
    if verb == "check" && has("audit") && ["cost","residency","fix","store","fixture","as","release","emit-lean","ack-notes"].iter().any(|f|has(f)) { return Err("E_INPUT: check --audit takes only --json or --verbose".into()); }
    if verb == "test" && has("verbose") && !has("residency") { return Err("E_INPUT: test --verbose requires --residency".into()); }
    if verb == "run" && has("token-env") && !has("release") { return Err("E_INPUT: run --token-env requires --release".into()); }
    if verb == "why" && has("instance") && crate::verbs::parse(args).positionals.first().map(String::as_str) != Some("recent") { return Err("E_INPUT: why --instance requires why recent".into()); }
    if verb == "token" && has("port") && has("release") { return Err("E_INPUT: token --port selects dev and cannot combine with --release".into()); }
    if verb == "token" && has("quiet") && has("json") { return Err("E_INPUT: token takes either --quiet or --json".into()); }
    Ok(())
}

fn retired(verb: &str, name: &str) -> bool {
    command(verb).is_some_and(|command|command.flags.iter().any(|flag|flag.name==name && flag.retired))
}
pub fn switch(name: &str) -> bool { name=="help" || commands().iter().flat_map(|command|&command.flags).any(|flag|flag.name==name && flag.value.is_empty()) }

#[cfg(test)]
mod integration_tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn wave9_web_flags_and_upgrade_keep_shared_validation() {
        let args = ["witness", "web", "dist", "--url", "http://127.0.0.1", "--as", "alice", "--email", "alice@example.test", "--password-env", "PRIVATE_PASSWORD", "--mutation", "add", "--args", "{}", "--timeout", "30", "--export", "dist"].map(String::from);
        assert!(validate(&args).is_ok());
        for verb in ["upgrade", "witness"] {
            assert!(validate(&[verb.into(), "--unknown-wave9".into()]).is_err());
        }
        assert!(validate(&["upgrade".into(), "0.4.25".into()]).is_ok());
        assert!(validate(&["upgrade".into(), "--to".into(), "0.4.25".into(), "--json".into()]).is_ok());
        assert!(validate(&["witness".into(), "web".into(), "--password-env".into()]).is_err());
    }
}

fn value(verb: &str, name: &str) -> String {
    command(verb).and_then(|command|command.flags.iter().find(|flag|flag.name==name)).filter(|flag|!flag.value.is_empty()).map(|flag|format!(" {}",flag.value)).unwrap_or_default()
}

/// Generated from the same flag table used before dispatch and side effects.
pub(crate) fn help(verb: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::from("Flags (validated before any project or store work):\n");
    for name in allowed(verb).split_whitespace().chain(["help"]) {
        let value = value(verb, name);
        let description = match (verb, name) {
            ("deploy", "schema") => " — deploy a saved complete source; keep it outside snapback/",
            ("deploy", "staging") => " — classify a new disposable deployment as staging; existing production stores refuse",
            ("dev", "unsafe-dev-doors") => " — explicitly open unauthenticated local fixture/clock/persona doors for password-only apps",
            ("test", "source") => " — test a saved complete source; keep it outside snapback/",
            ("doctor", "repair-expiry-orphan") => " — offline recovery of one physically missing expiring parent, table:id; stop its host and back up first (guide expire)",
            ("clock", "each-boundary") => " — fire every crossed schedule window in chronological order (default: latest window only)",
            (_, "help") => " — print this verb's help (-h also works)",
            _ if retired(verb,name) => " — retired spelling; the command explains its replacement before work",
            ("doctor", "instance") => " — inspect one dev instance's health and refusals",
            ("doctor", "url") => " — inspect the backend at this URL",
            ("doctor", "latency") => " — measure request and stream latency",
            ("doctor", "reset") => " — reset latency observations before measuring",
            ("witness", "password-env") => " — environment variable holding the sign-in password",
            ("witness", "background") => " — run the native witness in the background; inspect witness status",
            ("witness", "if-present") => " — skip an absent native witness installation",
